text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"@'\nfrom pathlib import Path\ncode=r'''import json,hashlib,pathlib\nP=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent\nmd=lambda s:hashlib.md5(s.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()\nq=lambda v:'NULL' if v is None else \"'\"+str(v).replace(\"'\",\"''\")+\"'\"\nload=lambda name:json.loads((P/name).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\ndef rows(obj): return obj.get('structuredContent',obj)['rows']\nedits=load('payload-approved.json')\nassert len(edits)==10 and len({(e['table'],e['row_id'],e['column']) for e in edits})==10\nfor e in edits:\n assert md(e['old'])==e['old_md5'] and md(e['new'])==e['new_md5']\n assert '\\r' not in e['new']\nsources={x['qid']:x for x in rows(load('initial-live-snapshot.json')['sources'])}\nsources.update({x['qid']:x for x in rows(load('additional-source-live.json'))})\nneeded={e['qid'] for e in edits};assert needed<=sources.keys(),needed-sources.keys()\nqvals=[];cvals=[]\nfor qid in sorted(needed):\n s=sources[qid];qvals.append('('+','.join(q(v) for v in [qid,s['id'],md(s['stimulus']),md(s['prompt']),s['correct_choice']])+')')\n assert len(s['choices'])==5 and sorted(c['letter'] for c in s['choices'])==list('ABCDE')\n for c in s['choices']:\n  text=c.get('choice_text',c.get('text'));h=md(text) if text is not None else c['md5']\n  cvals.append('('+','.join(q(v) for v in [qid,c['id'],c['letter'],h])+','+('true' if c['letter']==s['correct_choice'] else 'false')+')')\nevals=[]\nfor e in edits:\n evals.append('('+','.join(q(v) for v in [e['qid'],e['table'],e['column'],e['row_id'],e.get('explanation_id'),str(e.get('position',e.get('letter',''))),e.get('label'),e['old'],e['old_md5'],e['new'],e['new_md5']])+')')\nstandard=next(x for x in rows(load('q157-styles-live.json')) if x['style']=='standard')['notes_d'][0]\nsql='with expected_questions(qid,rid,stimulus_md5,prompt_md5,key) as (values\\n'+',\\n'.join(qvals)+'),\\nexpected_choices(qid,rid,letter,body_md5,correct) as (values\\n'+',\\n'.join(cvals)+'),\\nexpected_edits(qid,tbl,col,rid,eid,slot,label,old_text,old_md5,new_text,new_md5) as (values\\n'+',\\n'.join(evals)+'''),\nlocked_questions as materialized(select t.* from lr_questions t where t.qid in(select qid from expected_questions) for update),\nlocked_choices as materialized(select t.* from lr_answer_choices t where t.question_id in(select id from locked_questions) for update),\nlocked_explanations as materialized(select t.* from lr_explanations t where t.question_id in(select id from locked_questions) for update),\nlocked_steps as materialized(select t.* from lr_explanation_steps t where t.id::text in(select rid from expected_edits where tbl='lr_explanation_steps') for update),\nlocked_notes as materialized(select t.* from lr_explanation_choice_notes t where t.id::text in(select rid from expected_edits where tbl='lr_explanation_choice_notes') or t.id::text='''+q(standard['id'])+''' for update),\nactual as (\n select q.qid,'lr_questions'::text tbl,'stimulus'::text col,q.id::text rid,null::text eid,''::text slot,null::text label,q.stimulus body from locked_questions q\n union all select q.qid,'lr_answer_choices','tag',c.id::text,null,c.letter::text,null,c.tag from locked_choices c join locked_questions q on q.id=c.question_id\n union all select q.qid,'lr_explanation_steps','body',s.id::text,e.id::text,s.position::text,s.label,s.body from locked_steps s join locked_explanations e on e.id=s.explanation_id join locked_questions q on q.id=e.question_id\n union all select q.qid,'lr_explanation_choice_notes','body',n.id::text,e.id::text,n.letter::text,null,n.body from locked_notes n join locked_explanations e on e.id=n.explanation_id join locked_questions q on q.id=e.question_id),\neligibility as materialized(select\n not exists(select 1 from expected_questions x where (select count(*) from locked_questions t where t.id::text=x.rid and t.qid=x.qid and md5(t.stimulus)=x.stimulus_md5 and md5(t.prompt)=x.prompt_md5 and t.correct_choice::text=x.key)<>1)\n and not exists(select 1 from expected_choices x where (select count(*) from locked_choices c join locked_questions q on q.id=c.question_id where q.qid=x.qid and c.id::text=x.rid and c.letter::text=x.letter and md5(c.choice_text)=x.body_md5 and c.is_correct=x.correct)<>1)\n and not exists(select 1 from locked_choices c join locked_questions q on q.id=c.question_id where not exists(select 1 from expected_choices x where x.qid=q.qid and x.rid=c.id::text))\n and not exists(select 1 from expected_edits e where (select count(*) from actual a where a.qid=e.qid and a.tbl=e.tbl and a.col=e.col and a.rid=e.rid and (e.eid is null or a.eid=e.eid) and a.slot=e.slot and (e.label is null or a.label=e.label) and a.body=e.old_text and md5(a.body)=e.old_md5)<>1)\n and (select count(*) from locked_notes n where n.id::text='''+q(standard['id'])+' and md5(n.body)='+q(standard['md5'])+''')=1 eligible)'''\n(P/'preflight.sql').write_bytes((sql+'\\nselect eligible from eligibility;\\n').encode('utf-8'))\nwrite_ctes=[]\nfor i,e in enumerate(edits):\n ownership='t.qid='+q(e['qid']) if e['table']=='lr_questions' else 'exists(select 1 from locked_questions q where q.id=t.question_id and q.qid='+q(e['qid'])+')' if e['table']=='lr_answer_choices' else 't.explanation_id='+q(e['explanation_id'])+'::uuid and exists(select 1 from locked_explanations x join locked_questions q on q.id=x.question_id where x.id=t.explanation_id and q.qid='+q(e['qid'])+')'\n if e['table']=='lr_explanation_steps':ownership+=' and t.position='+str(e['position'])+' and t.label='+q(e['label'])\n if e['table'] in ['lr_answer_choices','lr_explanation_choice_notes']:ownership+=' and t.letter::text='+q(e['letter'])\n write_ctes.append('u'+str(i)+' as (update '+e['table']+' t set '+e['column']+'='+q(e['new'])+' from eligibility g where g.eligible and t.id='+q(e['row_id'])+'::uuid and '+ownership+' and t.'+e['column']+'='+q(e['old'])+' and md5(t.'+e['column']+')='+q(e['old_md5'])+' returning t.id::text rid,'+q(e['table'])+'::text tbl,md5(t.'+e['column']+') body_md5)')\nsql+=',\\n'+',\\n'.join(write_ctes)+',\\nwrites as ('+' union all '.join('select * from u'+str(i) for i in range(len(edits)))+'''), result as(select (select eligible from eligibility) eligible,(select count(*) from writes) updated_rows,(select count(*) from writes w join expected_edits e on e.rid=w.rid and e.tbl=w.tbl and e.new_md5=w.body_md5) verified_rows,(select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(w)) from writes w) receipts)\nselect *,1/(case when (not eligible and updated_rows=0) or (eligible and updated_rows=10 and verified_rows=10) then 1 else 0 end) atomic_assertion from result;\n'''\n(P/'apply-atomic.sql').write_bytes(sql.encode('utf-8'))\nprint('Built guarded10-edit atomic SQL and read-only preflight; all files LF-only')\n'''\nPath('corpus/lr-explanations/fixes/step4-2026-10-10/build-atomic.py').write_bytes(code.encode('utf-8'))\nprint('generator Typically, if a Most Supported paragraph ends with a Pivot sentence, the correct answer represents a safely worded way of resolving the tension created by the pivot word. We call that sort of pattern Reconcile the Pivot.\\\\nThe tension here is that Professor Bums thinks that new observations refute old observations, but the speaker is pointing out that these new observations occurred under poor conditions.\\\\nThis seems to suggest that the new data points aren\'t super trustworthy. Would we be a little hasty to toss out the old observations as invalid, based off some sketchy new observations?\\", \\"id\\": \\"8c372d18-2721-49a8-b820-0b191e9d476a\\", \\"label\\": \\"Evaluate\\", \\"md5\\": \\"f88c029f45ba3361ea5a8cb60db2c30e\\", \\"position\\": 1}, {\\"body\\": \\"The wrong answers will probably go overboard and say, \\\\\\"Thus, the recent observations are worthless. edit['column'] + '=' + quote(edit['old']) + ' and md5(t.' + edit['column'] + ')=' + quote(edit['old_md5']) + ' returning t.id::text rid,' + quote(edit['table']) + '::text tbl,md5(t.' + edit['column'] verified_rows,\n+ (select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(w)) from writes w) receipts)\n+select *,1/(case when (not eligible and updated_rows=0) or (eligible and updated_rows=16 and verified_rows=16) then 1 else 0 'pdf-choice-proposals.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n+snapshot = json.loads((ROOT / 'initial-live-snapshot.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n+sources = {r['qid']: r for r in snapshot['sources']['structuredContent']['rows']}\n+assert len(edits) == len({e['row_id'] for e in edits}) == 2\n+question_values, choice_values, edit_values = [], [], []\n+for edit in edits:\n+    assert edit['table'] == 'lr_answer_choices' and edit['column'] == 'choice_text'\n+    assert md5(edit['old']) == edit['old_md5']\n+    assert '\\r' not in edit['new']\n+    edit['new_md5'] = md5(edit['new'])\n+    source = sources[edit['qid']]\n+    assert source['id'] == edit['question_id'] and source['correct_choice'] == edit['key']\n+    assert edit['letter'] != edit['key']\n+    question_values.append([edit['qid'], source['id'], md5(source['stimulus']), md5(source['prompt']), source['correct_choice']])\n+    assert sorted(c['letter'] for c in source['choices']) == list('ABCDE')\n+    for choice in source['choices']:\n+        if choice['id'] == edit['row_id']:\n+            assert choice['letter'] == edit['letter'] and choice['text'] == edit['old']\n+        choice_values.append([edit['qid'], choice['id'], choice['letter'], md5(choice['text']), str(choice['letter'] == source['correct_choice']).lower()])\n+    edit_values.append([edit['qid'], edit['question_id'], edit['row_id'], edit['letter'], edit['old'], edit['old_md5'], edit['new'], edit['new_md5']])\n+\n+sql = 'with expected_questions(qid,rid,stimulus_md5,prompt_md5,key) as (values\\n' + values(question_values) + '),\\n'\n+sql += 'expected_choices(qid,rid,letter,body_md5,correct) as (values\\n' + values(choice_values) + '),\\n'\n+sql += 'expected_edits(qid,qid_uuid,rid,letter,old_text,old_md5,new_text,new_md5) as (values\\n' + values(edit_values) + '),\\n'\n+sql += \"\"\"\n+locked_questions as materialized(select q.* from lr_questions q where q.qid in(select qid from expected_questions) for update),\n+locked_choices as materialized(select c.* from lr_answer_choices c where c.question_id in(select id from locked_questions) for update),\n+eligibility as materialized(select\n+ not exists(select 1 from expected_questions x where (select count(*) from locked_questions q where q.id::text=x.rid and q.qid=x.qid and md5(q.stimulus)=x.stimulus_md5 and md5(q.prompt)=x.prompt_md5 and q.correct_choice::text=x.key)<>1)\n+ and not exists(select 1 from expected_choices x where (select count(*) from locked_choices c join locked_questions q on q.id=c.question_id where q.qid=x.qid and c.id::text=x.rid and c.letter::text=x.letter and md5(c.choice_text)=x.body_md5 and c.is_correct=x.correct::boolean)<>1)\n+ and not exists(select 1 from locked_choices c where not exists(select 1 from expected_choices x where x.rid=c.id::text))\n+ and not exists(select 1 from expected_edits x where (select count(*) from locked_choices c join locked_questions q on q.id=c.question_id where q.qid=x.qid and q.id::text=x.qid_uuid and c.id::text=x.rid and c.letter::text=x.letter and c.choice_text=x.old_text and md5(c.choice_text)=x.old_md5)<>1) eligible)\n+\"\"\"\n+(ROOT / 'pdf-preflight.sql').write_bytes((sql + 'select eligible from eligibility;\\n').encode('utf-8'))\n+sql += \"\"\",\n+updated as(update lr_answer_choices t set choice_text=e.new_text from expected_edits e,eligibility g\n+ where g.eligible and t.id=e.rid::uuid and t.question_id=e.qid_uuid::uuid and t.letter::text=e.letter\n+ and t.choice_text=e.old_text and md5(t.choice_text)=e.old_md5\n+ and exists(select 1 from locked_questions q where q.id=t.question_id and q.qid=e.qid)\n+ returning t.id::text rid,md5(t.choice_text) body_md5),\n+result as(select (select eligible from eligibility) eligible,(select count(*) from updated) updated_rows,\n+ (select count(*) from updated u join expected_edits e on e.rid=u.rid and e.new_md5=u.body_md5) verified_rows,\n+ (select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(u)) from updated u) receipts)\n+select *,1/(case when (not eligible and updated_rows=0) or (eligible and updated_rows=2 and verified_rows=2) then 1 else 0 end) atomic_assertion from result;\n+\"\"\"\n+(ROOT / 'pdf-apply-atomic.sql').write_bytes(sql.encode('utf-8'))\n+\n+for edit in edits:\n+    next(c for c in choice_values if c[1] == edit['row_id'])[3] = edit['new_md5']\n+audit = 'with expected_questions(qid,rid,stimulus_md5,prompt_md5,key) as (values\\n' + values(question_values) + '),\\n'\n+audit += 'expected_choices(qid,rid,letter,body_md5,correct) as (values\\n' + values(choice_values) + '),\\n'\n+audit += 'expected_edits(qid,qid_uuid,rid,letter,old_text,old_md5,new_text,new_md5) as (values\\n' + values(edit_values) + '),\\n'\n+audit += \"\"\"\n+question_checks as(select x.qid,\n+ (select count(*) from lr_questions q where q.qid=x.qid and q.id::text=x.rid and md5(q.stimulus)=x.stimulus_md5 and md5(q.prompt)=x.prompt_md5 and q.correct_choice::text=x.key) matches from expected_questions x),\n+choice_checks as(select x.qid,x.letter,\n+ (select count(*) from lr_answer_choices c join lr_questions q on q.id=c.question_id where q.qid=x.qid and c.id::text=x.rid and c.letter::text=x.letter and md5(c.choice_text)=x.body_md5 and c.is_correct=x.correct::boolean) matches from expected_choices x),\n+edit_checks as(select x.qid,x.rid,x.letter,\n+ (select count(*) from lr_answer_choices c join lr_questions q on q.id=c.question_id where q.qid=x.qid and q.id::text=x.qid_uuid and c.id::text=x.rid and c.letter::text=x.letter and c.choice_text=x.new_text and md5(c.choice_text)=x.new_md5) matches from expected_edits x)\n+select jsonb_build_object('expected_rows',2,'matching_rows',(select count(*) from edit_checks where matches=1),\n+ 'edit_mismatches',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(c)) from edit_checks c where matches<>1),'[]'::jsonb),\n+ 'question_mismatches',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(c)) from question_checks c where matches<>1),'[]'::jsonb),\n+ 'choice_mismatches',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(c)) from choice_checks c where matches<>1),'[]'::jsonb),\n+ 'extra_choices',(select count(*) from lr_answer_choices c join lr_questions q on q.id=c.question_id where q.qid in(select qid from expected_questions) and not exists(select 1 from expected_choices x where x.rid=c.id::text))) audit;\n+\"\"\"\n+(ROOT / 'pdf-read-after-audit.sql').write_bytes(audit.encode('utf-8'))\n+(ROOT / 'pdf-payload-final.json').write_bytes((json.dumps(edits, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\\n').encode('utf-8'))\n+for name in ['pdf-preflight.sql', 'pdf-apply-atomic.sql', 'pdf-read-after-audit.sql']:\n+    assert b'\\r' not in (ROOT / name).read_bytes()\n+print('Built PDF two-choice atomic apply, preflight and audit; escaped E literals and LF bytes')\n*** End Patch"));
text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"python -I corpus/lr-explanations/fixes/step4-2026-10-10/build-pdf-choices.py","max_output_tokens":1000}));

text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"@'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport json,hashlib,re\np=Path('corpus/lr-explanations/fixes/step4-2026-10-10');row=json.loads((p/'pt132-live-snapshot.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['structuredContent']['rows'][0];newsteps={0:'Medications with an unpleasant taste are generally produced only in tablet, capsule, or soft-gel form.\\n\\nThe active ingredient in medication M is a waxy substance that cannot tolerate the heat used to manufacture tablets because it has a low melting point.\\n\\nThe company developing M does not have soft-gel manufacturing technology and manufactures all its medications itself.',1:'M will most likely be produced in capsule form.',2:'The argument narrows the options. M cannot be a tablet, because its waxy ingredient cannot tolerate the heat. M cannot be a soft-gel, because the company lacks that technology and makes all its medications itself.\\n\\nWhat would make capsule the likely choice? Medications with an unpleasant taste generally come in one of those three forms. But nothing tells us M has an unpleasant taste. Without that connection, the usual pattern gives us no reason to expect M to come in one of the three forms.\\n\\nIf M tastes unpleasant, the pattern applies to it. Two of the usual forms are ruled out, which supports capsule as the most likely remaining form. Notice the limit: \"generally\" allows exceptions. We are supporting \"most likely,\" not proving that a capsule is the only possible outcome.',3:'The stem asks what would most strongly support the conclusion if assumed. Look for the fact that connects M to the usual three-form pattern: M has an unpleasant taste. Then ruling out tablet and soft-gel gives us a reason to expect capsule. It strengthens the conclusion without turning \"most likely\" into a guarantee.'};newnotes={'B':'This connects M to the general pattern. If M has an unpleasant taste, we have a reason to expect tablet, capsule, or soft-gel. The ingredient rules out tablet, and the company cannot make soft-gels, so capsule becomes the likely choice.\\n\\nKeep the qualification: the premise says \"generally,\" so the answer supports \"most likely\" rather than guaranteeing capsule. When an argument narrows the usual options, check that the case fits the group described.','C':'This says a capsule medication is not also a soft-gel (C → ~SG). We already know M will not be a soft-gel, so this adds nothing. It also does not establish that M fits the general three-form pattern, because nothing says M tastes unpleasant.'};edits=[]\nfor table,key,newmap,slot in [('lr_explanation_steps','steps',newsteps,'position'),('lr_explanation_choice_notes','notes',newnotes,'letter')]:\n for r in row[key]:\n  if r[slot] not in newmap:continue\n  new=newmap[r[slot]];assert not re.search(r'\\b(he|she|his|her|him|hers|himself|herself)\\b|[—–]',new,re.I);assert hashlib.md5(r['body'].encode()).hexdigest()==r['md5'];e={'qid':row['qid'],'table':table,'column':'body','row_id':r['id'],'explanation_id':row['explanation_id'],'style':row['style'],slot:r[slot],'old':r['body'],'old_md5':r['md5'],'new':new,'new_md5':hashlib.md5(new.encode()).hexdigest(),'rationale':'Preserve generally/most likely; remove deterministic arrow or guarantee language. Keep Strengthen label and credited B unchanged.','source':'pt132-live-snapshot.json'}\n  if slot=='position':e['label']=r['label']\n  edits.append(e)\n(p/'pt132-proposals.json').write_bytes((json.dumps(edits,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\\n').encode());print(len(edits),'proposed modality corrections; one live style single; unchanged D/A/E notes')\n'@ | python -I -","max_output_tokens":1000}));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/corpus/lr-explanations/fixes/step4-2026-10-10/build-pt132.py\n+import hashlib\n+import json\n+from pathlib import Path\n+\n+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n+\n+\n+def md5(value):\n+    return hashlib.md5(value.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()\n+\n+\n+def quote(value):\n+    if value is None:\n+        return 'NULL'\n+    escaped = str(value).replace('\\\\', '\\\\\\\\').replace(\"'\", \"''\")\n+    return \"E'\" + escaped.replace('\\r', '\\\\r').replace('\\n', '\\\\n') + \"'\"\n+\n+\n+def values(records):\n+    return ',\\n'.join('(' + ','.join(quote(v) for v in row) + ')' for row in records)\n+\n+\n+review = json.loads((ROOT / 'reviewer-pt132.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n+assert review.get('verdict') == 'APPROVE'\n+edits = json.loads((ROOT / 'pt132-proposals.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n+snapshot = json.loads((ROOT / 'pt132-live-snapshot.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['structuredContent']['rows']\n+assert len(snapshot) == 1 and len(edits) == 7\n+source = snapshot[0]\n+assert source['qid'] == 'PT132-S2-Q3' and source['correct_choice'] == 'B'\n+assert source['question_type'] == 'Strengthen' and source['style'] == 'single'\n+by_id = {e['row_id']: e for e in edits}\n+assert len(by_id) == 7\n+body_values = []\n+for kind, key, slot in [('step', 'steps', 'position'), ('note', 'notes', 'letter')]:\n+    for row in source[key]:\n+        edit = by_id.get(row['id'])\n+        assert md5(row['body']) == row['md5']\n+        if edit:\n+            assert edit['old'] == row['body'] and edit['old_md5'] == row['md5']\n+            assert md5(edit['new']) == edit['new_md5']\n+            assert edit['explanation_id'] == source['explanation_id']\n+            assert edit[slot] == row[slot]\n+            assert '\\r' not in edit['new']\n+        body_values.append([kind, row['id'], str(row[slot]), row.get('label'), row['body'], row['md5'], edit['new'] if edit else row['body'], edit['new_md5'] if edit else row['md5'], str(edit is not None).lower()])\n+assert len(body_values) == 9\n+choice_values = [[c['id'], c['letter'], md5(c['choice_text']), str(c['is_correct']).lower()] for c in source['choices']]\n+assert sorted(c[1] for c in choice_values) == list('ABCDE')\n+question_values = [[source['question_id'], source['qid'], md5(source['stimulus']), md5(source['prompt']), source['correct_choice'], source['question_type'], source['explanation_id'], source['style']]]\n+sql = 'with question_expected(rid,qid,stimulus_md5,prompt_md5,key,qtype,eid,style) as (values\\n' + values(question_values) + '),\\n'\n+sql += 'choice_expected(rid,letter,body_md5,correct) as (values\\n' + values(choice_values) + '),\\n'\n+sql += 'body_expected(kind,rid,slot,label,old_text,old_md5,new_text,new_md5,target) as (values\\n' + values(body_values) + '),\\n'\n+sql += \"\"\"\n+locked_question as materialized(select q.* from lr_questions q where q.qid=E'PT132-S2-Q3' for update),\n+locked_explanation as materialized(select e.* from lr_explanations e where e.question_id in(select id from locked_question) for update),\n+locked_choices as materialized(select c.* from lr_answer_choices c where c.question_id in(select id from locked_question) for update),\n+locked_steps as materialized(select s.* from lr_explanation_steps s where s.explanation_id in(select id from locked_explanation) for update),\n+locked_notes as materialized(select n.* from lr_explanation_choice_notes n where n.explanation_id in(select id from locked_explanation) for update),\n+actual_bodies as (\n+ select 'step'::text kind,s.id::text rid,s.position::text slot,s.label,s.body from locked_steps s\n+ union all select 'note',n.id::text,n.letter::text,null::text,n.body from locked_notes n),\n+eligibility as materialized(select\n+ (select count(*) from locked_question)=1 and (select count(*) from locked_explanation)=1\n+ and (select count(*) from locked_choices)=5 and (select count(*) from actual_bodies)=9\n+ and (select count(*) from locked_question q join locked_explanation e on e.question_id=q.id join question_expected x on x.rid=q.id::text where q.qid=x.qid and md5(q.stimulus)=x.stimulus_md5 and md5(q.prompt)=x.prompt_md5 and q.correct_choice::text=x.key and q.question_type=x.qtype and e.id::text=x.eid and e.style=x.style)=1\n+ and not exists(select 1 from choice_expected x where (select count(*) from locked_choices c where c.id::text=x.rid and c.letter::text=x.letter and md5(c.choice_text)=x.body_md5 and c.is_correct=x.correct::boolean)<>1)\n+ and not exists(select 1 from body_expected x where (select count(*) from actual_bodies a where a.kind=x.kind and a.rid=x.rid and a.slot=x.slot and (x.label is null or a.label=x.label) and a.body=x.old_text and md5(a.body)=x.old_md5)<>1) eligible)\n+\"\"\"\n+(ROOT / 'pt132-preflight.sql').write_bytes((sql + 'select eligible from eligibility;\\n').encode('utf-8'))\n+sql += \"\"\",\n+us as(update lr_explanation_steps t set body=x.new_text from body_expected x,question_expected q,eligibility g\n+ where g.eligible and x.target::boolean and x.kind=E'step' and t.id=x.rid::uuid and t.explanation_id=q.eid::uuid\n+ and t.position::text=x.slot and t.label=x.label and t.body=x.old_text and md5(t.body)=x.old_md5\n+ returning t.id::text rid,md5(t.body) body_md5),\n+un as(update lr_explanation_choice_notes t set body=x.new_text from body_expected x,question_expected q,eligibility g\n+ where g.eligible and x.target::boolean and x.kind=E'note' and t.id=x.rid::uuid and t.explanation_id=q.eid::uuid\n+ and t.letter::text=x.slot and t.body=x.old_text and md5(t.body)=x.old_md5\n+ returning t.id::text rid,md5(t.body) body_md5),\n+writes as(select * from us union all select * from un),\n+result as(select (select eligible from eligibility) eligible,(select count(*) from us) updated_steps,(select count(*) from un) updated_notes,\n+ (select count(*) from writes) updated_rows,(select count(*) from writes w join body_expected x on x.rid=w.rid and x.new_md5=w.body_md5) verified_rows,\n+ (select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(w)) from writes w) receipts)\n+select *,1/(case when (not eligible and updated_rows=0) or (eligible and updated_rows=7 and verified_rows=7 and updated_steps=4 and updated_notes=3) then 1 else 0 end) atomic_assertion from result;\n+\"\"\"\n+(ROOT / 'pt132-apply-atomic.sql').write_bytes(sql.encode('utf-8'))\n+\n+audit = 'with question_expected(rid,qid,stimulus_md5,prompt_md5,key,qtype,eid,style) as (values\\n' + values(question_values) + '),\\n'\n+audit += 'choice_expected(rid,letter,body_md5,correct) as (values\\n' + values(choice_values) + '),\\n'\n+audit += 'body_expected(kind,rid,slot,label,old_text,old_md5,new_text,new_md5,target) as (values\\n' + values(body_values) + '),\\n'\n+audit += \"\"\"\n+question_check as(select count(*) matches from lr_questions q join question_expected x on q.id::text=x.rid join lr_explanations e on e.question_id=q.id where q.qid=x.qid and md5(q.stimulus)=x.stimulus_md5 and md5(q.prompt)=x.prompt_md5 and q.correct_choice::text=x.key and q.question_type=x.qtype and e.id::text=x.eid and e.style=x.style),\n+choice_checks as(select x.rid,x.letter,(select count(*) from lr_answer_choices c,question_expected q where c.id::text=x.rid and c.question_id=q.rid::uuid and c.letter::text=x.letter and md5(c.choice_text)=x.body_md5 and c.is_correct=x.correct::boolean) matches from choice_expected x),\n+actual_bodies as(select 'step'::text kind,s.id::text rid,s.position::text slot,s.label,s.body from lr_explanation_steps s,question_expected q where s.explanation_id=q.eid::uuid\n+ union all select 'note',n.id::text,n.letter::text,null::text,n.body from lr_explanation_choice_notes n,question_expected q where n.explanation_id=q.eid::uuid),\n+body_checks as(select x.kind,x.rid,x.slot,x.target,(select count(*) from actual_bodies a where a.kind=x.kind and a.rid=x.rid and a.slot=x.slot and (x.label is null or a.label=x.label) and a.body=x.new_text and md5(a.body)=x.new_md5) matches from body_expected x)\n+select jsonb_build_object('expected_edited_rows',7,'matching_edited_rows',(select count(*) from body_checks where target::boolean and matches=1),\n+ 'preserved_notes',(select count(*) from body_checks where not target::boolean and matches=1),\n+ 'body_mismatches',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(c)) from body_checks c where matches<>1),'[]'::jsonb),\n+ 'choice_mismatches',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(c)) from choice_checks c where matches<>1),'[]'::jsonb),\n+ 'question_snapshot_matches',(select matches from question_check),\n+ 'explanation_count',(select count(*) from lr_explanations e,question_expected q where e.question_id=q.rid::uuid),\n+ 'choice_count',(select count(*) from lr_answer_choices c,question_expected q where c.question_id=q.rid::uuid),\n+ 'body_count',(select count(*) from actual_bodies)) audit;\n+\"\"\"\n+(ROOT / 'pt132-read-after-audit.sql').write_bytes(audit.encode('utf-8'))\n+for name in ['pt132-preflight.sql', 'pt132-apply-atomic.sql', 'pt132-read-after-audit.sql']:\n+    assert b'\\r' not in (ROOT / name).read_bytes()\n+print('Built approved PT132 seven-body atomic SQL, full source guards, preserved D/E audit, escaped E literals')\n*** End Patch"));
text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"python -I corpus/lr-explanations/fixes/step4-2026-10-10/build-pt132.py","max_output_tokens":1000}));
